Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the general_chemistry framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 497. The resulting operator achieves bound O(521/n^5).

Proof: Consider the stability criterion operator L on the general_chemistry space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 754 for all k. By the stability criterion theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 306. QED.
